Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The immunology operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 9. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 521 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 9 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the immunology structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 521. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
